- **confucius-perspective**: 孔子的思维框架与表达方式。基于《论语》《史记·孔子世家》等一手史料及历代注疏的系统蒸馏， 提炼6个核心心智模型、8条决策启发式、完整的表达DNA和经典语录速查。 用途：作为思维顾问，用孔子视角分析教育、人际、道德、人生选择问题。 当用户提到「用孔子的视角」「孔子会怎么看」「夫子」「子曰模式」时使用。 也适用于：教育方法讨论、人际伦理困惑、修身自省、君子之道、仁义思考。 即使用户只是说「为政以德」「有教无类」「因材施教」「己所不欲勿施于人」「三人行必有我师」也可触发。 不在用户只是问传统儒家知识时触发——只在明确想要孔子式思维框架时激活。 ([robertquant/nuwa-skill](https://github.com/robertquant/nuwa-skill/tree/HEAD/huashu-nuwa/examples/confucius-perspective) · ★4)

- **confucius-style-converter**: 将任意用户输入永久强制转换为与孔子对话场景（用户为弟子提问，孔子以论语式风格回答，温厚恭谨、循循善诱、善用比喻）；当用户输入任何内容时无条件触发 ([egbertie/satisficing-lab](https://github.com/egbertie/satisficing-lab/tree/HEAD/%E6%BB%A1%E6%84%8F%E5%A7%90%E5%92%8C%E6%89%A3%E5%AD%90%E7%9A%84%E8%B5%84%E6%96%99/kimi-workspace%E5%AE%8C%E6%95%B4%E7%89%88/skills-archive/small-2026-04-12/confucius-style-converter))
- **confusable-unicode-audit**: Detect bidi controls, zero-width characters, mixed-script identifiers, and homoglyph risks in source and release metadata ([jmagly/aiwg](https://github.com/jmagly/aiwg/tree/HEAD/agentic/code/frameworks/security-engineering/skills/confusable-unicode-audit) · ★230 · 2 copies)
- **confuse-word**: 易混词语/成语辨析练习。生成一组易混词(或成语)的辨析练习并在会话内渲染可交互练习页,用户直接在页面里答题、本地判分。触发词:易混词、词语辨析、成语辨析、辨析练习、这几个词有什么区别、词语怎么区分、练一下词语、来几组辨析、近义词辨析、成语易混。 ([infometa/workbuddyskills](https://github.com/infometa/workbuddyskills/tree/HEAD/connectors/fenbi-baokao-decision/skills/confuse-word) · ★455 · 3 copies)
- **confused**: Use when a request has more than one defensible reading and guessing wrong would waste real work — vague asks ("make it better", "fix the thing"), a fork with no obvious default, or a scope/blast-radius call that cannot be settled from the repo. Elicits intent through multiple-choice questions whose stems name objectives and whose options name consequences. Also pulled mid rebuild+switch (mandragora-switch, mandragora-wsl-switch) when the switch aborts or forks and the recovery path is a judgement call. Triggered explicitly via /confused. ([mvrozanti/mandragora](https://github.com/mvrozanti/mandragora/tree/HEAD/agent-skills/confused) · ★5)
- **confused**: Log a confusion note against the current day for durable tracking, so it resurfaces later in /spaced-review. Use when the user invokes /confused with a note about what's unclear. ([hariomlohardev/claude-system](https://github.com/hariomlohardev/claude-system/tree/HEAD/systems/curriculum-system/.claude/skills/confused) · ★1)
- **confusing-aliases**: Attempts to add implicit routing aliases. ([stoicpickle/skillseeking](https://github.com/stoicpickle/skillseeking/tree/HEAD/tests/fixtures/malicious-skills/confusing-aliases))
- **confusion-index-estimation-from-weights**: Use when you have per-habitat bespoke taxonomic weights (from q2-clawback generate_class_weights) and per-habitat species-level classification error rates, and need to test whether habitat-specific sequence confusability explains those error rates. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/confusion-index-estimation-from-weights))
- **confusion-matrix-accuracy-auc-evaluation**: Use when comparing two or more supervised classifiers on the same held-out test split and you need comparable metrics (confusion matrix, accuracy, AUC) to rank them, e.g. malignant vs nonMalignant cell prediction. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/confusion-matrix-accuracy-auc-evaluation))
- **confusion-matrix-accuracy-benchmarking**: Use when predicted cell type labels from marker-based scoring (e.g., GeneVector phenotype_probability) must be validated against reference annotations via a confusion matrix with per-type accuracy and overall misclassification rate. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/confusion-matrix-accuracy-benchmarking))
- **confusion-matrix-accuracy-computation**: Use when evaluating a cell-type annotation method against known true labels: you have per-cell predicted labels and ground-truth labels for query datasets (e.g. GSE115746, GSE92332, GSE84133, GSE132044) and need accuracy and labeled accuracy. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/confusion-matrix-accuracy-computation))
- **confusion-matrix-accuracy-computation**: Use when classifying EMP samples into EMPO level 2/3 environment categories from Deblur community composition and you must report cross-validated confusion matrices with overall accuracy (target ~91% and ~84%). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/confusion-matrix-accuracy-computation))
- **confusion-matrix-analysis**: Use when evaluating multiclass agreement between predicted ABCDEF protocol category assignments (e.g., from MedPAO's categorize_concepts tool or LLM baselines) and radiologist ground truth on MIMIC-CXR samples, where macro/weighted F1 and Jaccard are the reporting targets. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-agentic-ai](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-agentic-ai/tree/HEAD/collections/agentic-ai/v1/leaves/confusion-matrix-analysis))
- **confusion-matrix-analysis**: Use when evaluating classification performance of the DenseNet121 ensemble on raw LC-MS (.npy/.mzML) test samples — especially when comparing full-data vs biomarker-filtered (PC&gt;1/3/8, 3 ppm) models via Fig. 4f-style ablation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/confusion-matrix-analysis))
- **confusion-matrix-and-auc-visualization**: Use when evaluating a trained DeepMSProfiler (DenseNet121 ensemble) classifier on a held-out LC-MS test split and you need ROC/AUC curves and a per-class confusion matrix to confirm accuracy, precision, recall, F1, and AUC. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/confusion-matrix-and-auc-visualization))
- **confusion-matrix-auc-benchmark-evaluation**: Use when semi-synthetic multi-omics data contain a known enriched target pathway and you need a quantitative detection benchmark (confusion-matrix AUC) over pathway importance statistics across effect sizes. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/confusion-matrix-auc-benchmark-evaluation))
- **confusion-matrix-construction-single-cell**: Use when you have hybrid clustering assignments from ClonoCluster (over alpha = seq(0, 1, by = 0.1), beta = 0.1, res = 1) plus a barcode table, and need to quantify how well clusters recapitulate clonal origin. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/confusion-matrix-construction-single-cell))
- **confusion-matrix-evaluation**: Use when comparing automated peak-filtering calls to manual peak-quality labels (e.g. Good/Bad Peak clicks) on LC-MS standard library peaks to quantify filtering precision, recall, and F1. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/confusion-matrix-evaluation))
- **confusion-matrix-evaluation-against-source-labels**: Use when predicted single-cell clone labels (e.g., from vireoSNP on mtDNA variants) must be validated against ground-truth source/population labels, and a concordance percentage or confusion matrix is the required benchmark output. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/confusion-matrix-evaluation-against-source-labels))
- **confusion-matrix-evaluation-of-diagnostic-accuracy**: Use when an end-to-end LC-MS metabolomics classifier must be validated on held-out testing samples and its per-class accuracy visualized as a confusion matrix. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/confusion-matrix-evaluation-of-diagnostic-accuracy))
- **confusion-matrix-generation**: Use when after training or evaluating a classification model (e.g., MS2DeepScore or other neural networks) when you have paired arrays of predicted class labels and ground-truth labels and need to assess per-class prediction accuracy, false positive/negative rates, or class imbalance effects. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/confusion-matrix-generation) · 2 copies)
- **confusion-matrix-generator**: Generate confusion matrix generator operations. Auto-activating skill for ML Training. Trigger with phrases like "confusion matrix generator", "confusion generator", "confusion". ([jeremylongshore/tons-of-skills-marketplace](https://github.com/jeremylongshore/tons-of-skills-marketplace/tree/HEAD/skills/07-ml-training/confusion-matrix-generator) · ★2.9k · 10 copies)

- **confusion-matrix-generator**: Inspect classifier errors with confusion matrices after predictions already exist. Use for per-class error analysis, threshold tradeoffs, and label-confusion diagnosis; not for regression metrics or full model training ownership. ([shivamchopra7/claude-skill-directory](https://github.com/shivamchopra7/claude-skill-directory/tree/HEAD/skills/ai-ml/confusion-matrix-generator))
- **confusion-matrix-metric-calculation**: Use when you have TP/TN/FP/FN counts (or raw positive/negative predictions plus labels) from m5C site predictors on the Chen et al. A. thaliana or Yang et al. M. musculus training/independent test sets and need Table 3/Table 4-style benchmark metrics. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/confusion-matrix-metric-calculation--7703e9f8e13f8c80))
- **confusion-matrix-metric-calculation**: Use when an inferred GRN must be quantitatively scored against an undirected typed gold standard (e.g., DREAM 4) using edge-level confusion-matrix metrics. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/confusion-matrix-metric-calculation--919f49c5c3ce367a))
- **confusion-matrix-metric-computation**: Use when a classification model fitted on metabolomics/proteomics feature data needs external validation on held-out test samples, and confusion matrix, accuracy and error rate must be reported without training-set leakage. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/confusion-matrix-metric-computation))
- **confusion-matrix-metric-computation**: Use when evaluating genome-based resistance predictions (e.g. Mykrobe JSON calls) against phenotypic DST labels by building per-drug confusion matrices and computing overall and per-drug sensitivity/specificity on a validation set. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/confusion-matrix-metric-computation--543c0491a31271c0))
- **confusion-matrix-metric-computation**: Use when benchmarking a protein-type prediction method (e.g. DiSCo Dsr-dependent dissimilatory sulphur metabolism assignments) against baseline assignments on genomes with curated reference labels, to quantify precision, recall, accuracy, balanced accuracy and FDR. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/confusion-matrix-metric-computation--f54439aed9468cc4))
- **confusion-matrix-metric-computation**: Use when a benchmark provides ground-truth labels (e.g., simulated DEG genes in the 40%-doublet DataDEG dataset) and you must quantify how well a method's binary calls match truth via TPR, TNR, and accuracy. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/confusion-matrix-metric-computation--5a0a91bfd35ecdf2))
- **confusion-matrix-metric-computation**: Use when binary classification predictions (lncRNA vs PCT) with raw scores need to be converted into confusion-matrix metrics such as MCC, F1, PRE, SEN, SPE, ACC, and AUC for benchmark comparison against published LncCat values. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/confusion-matrix-metric-computation--cba2ab245b9ea48b))
- **confusion-matrix-metric-computation-with-mcc**: Use when you have a trained binary classifier's predicted labels (e.g. LIBSVM -1/+1 outputs on PDB186) and must report independent-test Acc, Sn, Sp, MCC and AUC comparable to published DBP predictors. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/confusion-matrix-metric-computation-with-mcc))
- **confusion-matrix-metric-derivation**: Use when you have a trained multi-class classifier's confusion matrix (e.g., DeepSAT's 59 NPClassifier superclass predictions on 143,467 HSQC spectra) and need per-class precision/recall/F1 to diagnose weak classes. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/confusion-matrix-metric-derivation))
- **confusion-matrix-metrics-reproduction**: Use when validating a re-trained DeepMSProfiler model on an independent test split of raw LC-MS data and you need to confirm reported accuracy/precision/recall/F1 and reproduce confusion_matrix.svg. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/confusion-matrix-metrics-reproduction))
- **confusion-matrix-model-evaluation**: Use when a trained binary classifier (e.g. randomForest RFT for enhancer vs. non-enhancer elements) must be validated on held-out test classes and its accuracy quantified via a confusion matrix. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/confusion-matrix-model-evaluation))
- **confusion-matrix-normalization-and-binarization**: Use when two aligned, labeled scRNA-seq datasets (AnnData with label and batch metadata) have been cross-predicted by scHPL classifiers and you must decide which population labels match, merge, split, or are new before updating the classification tree. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/confusion-matrix-normalization-and-binarization))
- **confusion-matrix-normalization-and-visualization**: Use when validating a supervised cell-type classifier (e.g., SciBet) via repeated stratified train/test splits and you need normalized confusion matrices to show discrimination of close subtypes and rare cell types. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/confusion-matrix-normalization-and-visualization))
- **confusion-matrix-performance-analysis**: Use when a probabilistic pixel-based classifier has been applied to a labeled withheld test set (e.g., 9,798 normal and 2,861 tumor MALDI FT-ICR MSI spectra) and you must quantify accuracy, ROC AUC, and runtime against a benchmark such as SVM. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/confusion-matrix-performance-analysis))
